30-EVAL PLAN STUDY — 10 Tradeify + 10 Lucid + 10 TPT 50K, MNQ + MGC baskets, real 1-minute data 2020-06 → 2026-10
=====================================================================================================================
Model (as the user stated the rules): EOD trailing drawdown $2,000 (floor = best end-of-day balance − 2,000), target +$3,000,
consistency best day ≤ 50% of the total (a bigger day is not a breach: the target grows to 2 × best day), TPT passes one trading
day after reaching it (tiny trade). Fee assumed $120 per eval. Costs: $1 round trip per micro + 1 tick slippage per contract per side.
Basket = MNQ q1 + MGC q2 same direction, both entered on the same minute close, closed on the minute close that reaches +target /
−risk / the floor, or at 16:54 NY. A cohort = 30 evals started together; cohorts start every 5 sessions (≈ 345 cohorts); each runs
up to 40 sessions. Train = cohorts starting 2020–2023, test = 2024–2026. Program: scratchpad eval30/Program.cs (≈ 20 s per full run).
1,575 eval variants + 1,440 funded variants were run.

KEY FINDINGS
1. The firm rules decide the pass rate, not the trade idea. Every sensible plan lands at 26–31 % per eval (≈ 8 of 30).
   Random direction, −500/+1500, MNQ 4 + MGC 4, one basket a day at 9:30: 28.8 % (train 27.5 / test 30.4), 8.5 days to pass,
   $417 of fees per funded account. Of 30: median 8 passed (10th–90th pct 1–16), P(≥5) 71 %, P(≥10) 42 %.
2. Risk / target barely matters: −300…−1,500 against +1,000/+1,500 all give 26.6–28.8 %. Smaller risk = more days (11 vs 6).
   +3,000 targets are WORSE (19.8 %): a +3,000 day doubles the target under the 50 % rule.
3. Size: MNQ 4 + MGC 4 or MNQ 3 + MGC 6 best (28–29 %); 2+2 is the same rate but takes 15 days; aggressive 10+5 / 5+10 drop to 23 %
   (minute-close overshoot through the stop, costs); MGC-only 21 %. Aggression does not buy a higher pass rate, only fewer days.
4. One basket a day vs several a day (until +1,500 or 11:30): the same (28.8 vs 29.4 %); several a day passes in 6 days instead of 8.5.
5. COPY (all 30 on the same trade) vs each its own trade: the SAME average pass rate, but copy is all-or-nothing:
   P(≥5 of 30 pass) 29 % with copy vs 71–90 % with independent trades. Never put all 30 on one identical basket.
6. Entries that beat random in BOTH halves, same risk/size (56 matched settings each, pass-rate points over random):
     NY ORB 30m BREAK (9:30–10:00 MNQ range, enter on the first 1-min close beyond it until 11:30, in the break direction):
        +2.8 (train +2.6 / test +3.0), better in 52 of 56 settings
     LONDON MOVE CONTINUATION (at 9:30 trade the direction MNQ moved 3:00 → 9:30): +3.3 (train +1.8 / test +5.2), 50 of 56
     (its mirror, LONDON FADE, is −2.5 in both halves — the effect looks real, not luck)
     TREND 15m EMA200 at 10:00: +1.5 (+2.4 / +0.2) — weak; VWAP side, prior-day break, both-trends-agree: not consistent in test.
   These are SMALL edges (≈ 29 % → 32 %). The luck band for one signal path is ±4 points (random copy variants range 9–36 %).
7. Two-stage sizing (wide stop until the first win, tight after, or the reverse) and halving/raising size after a losing day:
   no reliable gain (all within ±1.5 points of the plain plan).
8. FUNDED TPT PRO (intraday trailing $2,000 incl. open profit, locks at the start; success = +$2,100 within 60 sessions):
   ≈ 30 % reach it with any sensible plan (−500/+1000, −750/+750, −1000/+2100). Intraday trailing costs ≈ 3.3 points vs the same
   plan under EOD trailing (open profit given back raises the floor). Scale-out (half at 50 %, rest to breakeven): −0.9 (no help).
   Up to 3 trades a day vs 1: +0.2 (no difference). Tiny targets (−250/+250) are bad (20.8 %): costs. MNQ-only 4 contracts 32.5 %,
   MNQ 6 + MGC 3 31.1 %, 4+4 29.5 %. NY ORB break as the entry: +1.8 points on average (train +0.1 / test +3.9) — not reliable.
   The best-looking rows (≈ 43 %) are the top of 1,440 noisy variants; plan on ≈ 30–33 %.

RECOMMENDED EVAL PLAN (highest pass rate that held in both halves, independent outcomes across the 30)
  • Each account: ONE basket a day, New York session. MNQ 4 + MGC 4 (or MNQ 3 + MGC 6). Stop −$500 to −$750, target +$1,500.
  • Direction: split the 30 into three groups so they do not all win / lose together:
      10 accounts = NY ORB 30m break (wait 10:00; enter on the first 1-min close beyond the 9:30–10:00 MNQ range, until 11:30; no break = no trade)
      10 accounts = London continuation at 9:30 (direction of MNQ from 3:00 to 9:30)
      10 accounts = 15m EMA200 trend at 10:00 (or each its own coin)
  • After +$1,500 on a day: stop that account for the day (the 50 % rule). After a loss: same size next day (reducing did not help).
  • When an account has +$3,000: stop; TPT accounts do one tiny trade the next day to pass.
  • Expectation for 30 evals: ≈ 9 pass (≈ 30 %), roughly 3–16 in practice; ≈ 7–10 trading days; ≈ $380–420 of fees per funded account.
  • Funded TPT PRO: one basket a day, MNQ 4 (MNQ-only) or MNQ 6 + MGC 3, −$500/+$1,000 or −$750/+$750, close at the target
    (no runners: open profit raises the intraday floor), stop for the day after the target or two losses. ≈ 30 % reach the
    +$2,100 lock; the rest blow. TPT forbids automation on PRO: these are manual clicks.

CAVEATS: closes of 1-minute bars (no intrabar wicks; exits overshoot both ways); slippage of 1 tick may be light at 9:30–10:00;
futures data (continuous contracts), no news filter; rules modelled as stated by the user (check Tradeify / Lucid fine print:
daily loss limits, min days, consistency definitions, payout rules); 3,000+ variants were tried, so the top single rows are
inflated — the matched comparisons in finding 6 are the trustworthy part.
